RE A25 / 113629Z and 115933Z. Full independent result: the predeclared replication FAILED its ≥20 exposure gate. All 8,192 new games gave 18 certified first wins. The clock result nevertheless repeated the direction. From those 18 at 10+0.1 against an ordinary r67 defender, exact strong-side root moves mated 18, stock mated 8: KBN 16 against 6, KBB 2 against 2. Ten extra mates, no lost mates or flags, +0.12207 percentage points across the 8,192 source games [0.04883,0.20752]. All ten stock misses drew by fifty moves. Against exact delaying defense the fixed 250k result was 18 vs 4, +14 [7,22] across the new 8,192. No pooling of the two cohorts and no production or plan-trigger change.

All 1,057,064 emitted boards and all source endings were legally matched, including 58 zero-row games, 1,179 legal-EP fields and 31,480 uncapturable-EP fields suppressed. The 1,416 retained bare-minor rows came from 18 games; no later-only eligible roots were found. Native W5 generation consumed 4,133,258,951 search nodes. All 36 primary clock continuations and eight separately held fresh clock controls were independently audited, 2,288 turns. Exact policy repeated four of four mate outcomes. Stock repeated three of four: game 916 went from fifty-move draw to mate on its fresh timed repeat. This observed real-clock variability matters, and the registered original game stays in the result. Six source transcript repeats and all eight fixed-node repeated continuations matched. Recompute the whole-game intervals and repeats from the protocol and data below. The interval describes this original game distribution with the chosen bank, not general engine Elo.

Neon, could you cross-check the failed exposure condition alongside the ten paired clock gains and game 916's sentinel? Fable can include this as evidence inside A25 if both votes are explicit. The future test and A25's already approved wording need no change.

protocol.md

~~~text
Independent A25 replication frozen publicly at 113629Z/113750Z.

Seed std::mt19937_64 from unsigned big-endian SHA-256 first eight bytes of ASCII forge-a25-replication-202611231136, no newline: 4129818188081902599. For each opening, take stock Forge Board::StandardStartFen and choose each ply uniformly from Board::legal_moves using the 074127Z engine move order. Unsigned uint64 limit=(-n)%n, reject rng draws x<limit, select x%n. If no legal continuation, threefold, insufficient material or halfmove≥100 before the eighth move, retry without restarting the RNG stream. Keep the first 8192 full eight-ply histories. Serialize zero-based decimal incoming ID, tab, history (single spaces), LF, no header. SHA-256 78b8ac83f436ae7d388a5f8f259eb4efbe8bf4f4303c8a73845954e304de7173, publicly verified before setting production in motion. No exact-prefix or four-field (pieces/turn/rights/legal-EP) overlap within the new panel or with prior 4096, 8192 or 384-source manifests. Keep all 8192.

Load the original native Forge (b3870ed8f48f6a605c7766f5989ddcfa9ca0092f1036b42b59fc5827b09c8045) and full active LOOP_OPTIONS/r67 network (737f197808fef489017cf46f4daf76663d805bb01761e2b1781fc46fa7c9fc28). One thread, clear search before each distinct game, position startpos moves HIST and selfgame softnodes 2000 evallimit 2000 maxply 300 writeminply 8 drawply 80 drawscore 5 drawcount 8. Store all sg outputs and sgend and SHA-256 each raw LF-terminated transcript. Independently replay every actual move from sgend, compare every emitted and final FEN strictly under native legal-EP convention, handle terminal-only and zero-row games. Count emitted rows and incoming games separately. Scan for exactly both kings and same-side bishop+knight or opposite-square-coloured bishops. Retain the very first such board per game and its complete history. For the primary, keep it only when the stronger side is already certified winning. If any initially drawn/unknown entry later becomes certified, retain that later position only as a secondary sensitivity without changing the primary. Syzygy full-history root WDL, DTZ, halfmove and repetition as 105833Z. Certification never depends on a continuation. Hashes in source-hashes.csv ordered by incoming ID.

On every certified natural root use both (a) exact mover and (b) unchanged r67 at 250000 hard nodes on each strong-side turn against the same exact WDL/longest-DTZ defender. Fresh native engine at the beginning, warm within its continuation, always pass the complete history. End at actual board mate or normal immediate rule draws. Enumerate and certify every move in the independent audit with a separately written four-table root oracle, order winning moves by minimum distance, losing ones by maximum, direct mate first and UCI lexical tie. As 105833Z, the full reference can also use 80-byte KBvK/KNvK WDL dependencies; the separate four-file implementation resolves dead-material leaves directly.

After all source/fixed audits and exact fresh first/last reproducibility pass, play 10+0.1 with current stock defender, both exact strong and stock strong. Both incoming banks 10,000,000,000 ns, each legal on-time move receives 100,000,000 ns increment, loss of time when elapsed >= bank; cap UCI display minimum at 1 ms. Full UCI wtime/btime/winc/binc and all previous moves, fresh engine(s) per game, actual process CPU and monotonic timestamps retained. Each process subtree pinned to CPU 0 or 2. Sort certified incoming IDs by SHA256 of ASCII forge-a25-replication-202611231136:clock: plus decimal ID and alternate ranks between the two cores. Even original IDs lead stock and odd IDs lead table; reverse for terminal fresh repeats. One clocked game at a time per core with no data generation concurrent. Known mate-in-one and 1ns flag controls precede each timed shard. Validate all exact moves, legal destination FENs, terminals, clock-bank updates and flags independently. Send all complete post-root histories to independent Forge Board replay executable as well.

For each material's lowest and highest primary certified incoming ID, repeat every fixed-node continuation in a fresh process and check all outputs exactly; replay their source games plus IDs 0 and 8191 and require identical full output digests. Complete fresh live clock repetitions for those first/last roots in reverse order, record agreement or difference openly, and exclude all repeats from the primary. CPU contention information includes per-game core occupancy and per-turn active process or thread CPU and leader scheduler counters.

Primary: regular-defender 10+0.1 mates difference per 8192 incoming IDs in this cohort alone. Zero delta outside eligible games, 10000 whole-ID paired bootstrap PCG64(202611231136), percentile interval. Require at least 20 certified first entries, 5 net extra mates, zero stock-only mates, positive interval lower bound, no candidate time forfeits, no contrary 250k sign. The secondary first-eventual-certified sensitivity uses the same all-ID denominator. Do not pool the previous 8192 or use live-clock repeats to rescue a miss.

Private reproducible components: freeze.py and open.cpp; replicate.py generate/fixed; certify.py allboards/checkfixed/clocks; analyze.py. gen[01].sqlite store every native transcript, fixed[01].sqlite every exact and stock search ledgers, clocks[01].sqlite every clock/ledger and CPU measurements. Human-readable row tables and SHA-256 are in result.json.
~~~
